{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Af1nESkfXNRSjBCZUUxzbH","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→//! Generic utilities commonly used across VisuTility Orchestrator.\n     2→//!\n     3→//! The abstractions in this module are intentionally framework-agnostic:\n     4→//! they do not depend on any particular CV/ML crate and can therefore be\n     5→//! shared by every layer (Ingress, Data-Ops, Feature-Ops, …).\n     6→//!\n     7→//! All functions are `#[inline]` where it makes sense to do so, and\n     8→//! significant branches are annotated with `#[cold]` to let the optimizer\n     9→//! keep hot-paths small.\n    10→\n    11→use std::{\n    12→    fmt,\n    13→    fs::File,\n    14→    io::Read,\n    15→    path::{Path, PathBuf},\n    16→    sync::atomic::{AtomicUsize, Ordering},\n    17→    time::{Duration, Instant},\n    18→};\n    19→\n    20→use anyhow::{anyhow, Context, Result};\n    21→use once_cell::sync::Lazy;\n    22→use parking_lot::RwLock;\n    23→use serde::de::DeserializeOwned;\n    24→use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};\n    25→use thiserror::Error;\n    26→use tracing::{debug, error, info, instrument};\n    27→\n    28→/// VisuTility's generic error type for utility-layer operations.\n    29→///\n    30→/// We prefer using [`anyhow::Error`] at the top-level application\n    31→/// boundary to allow for heterogeneous sources, but within the\n    32→/// utility layer we capture the most common failure categories so that\n    33→/// callers can still pattern-match when they need to.\n    34→#[derive(Debug, Error)]\n    35→pub enum UtilError {\n    36→    #[error(\"I/O error: {0}\")]\n    37→    Io(#[from] std::io::Error),\n    38→\n    39→    #[error(\"serialization error: {0}\")]\n    40→    Serde(#[from] serde_json::Error),\n    41→\n    42→    #[error(\"configuration not found at path `{0}`\")]\n    43→    ConfigNotFound(PathBuf),\n    44→\n    45→    #[error(\"task failed: {0}\")]\n    46→    TaskFailed(String),\n    47→}\n    48→\n    49→/// Generic wrapper around an [`Instant`] which logs the elapsed time at\n    50→/// **drop**.  The measurement is therefore *scoped*; as soon as the guard\n    51→/// goes out of scope, latency is emitted through the `tracing` facade.\n    52→///\n    53→/// ```no_run\n    54→/// use visu_tility_utils::ScopedTimer;\n    55→/// let _g = ScopedTimer::new(\"heavy_initialisation\");\n    56→/// // … some expensive code …\n    57→— guard drops here and we get a log line with\n    58→//   the elapsed time\n    59→/// ```\n    60→pub struct ScopedTimer<'a> {\n    61→    started: Instant,\n    62→    label: &'a str,\n    63→}\n    64→\n    65→impl<'a> ScopedTimer<'a> {\n    66→    /// Begin measuring elapsed time for the supplied label.\n    67→    #[inline]\n    68→    pub fn new(label: &'a str) -> Self {\n    69→        Self {\n    70→            started: Instant::now(),\n    71→            label,\n    72→        }\n    73→    }\n    74→\n    75→    /// Stop the timer and return the [`Duration`] since creation without\n    76→    /// logging. This allows manual control when automatic logging at `drop`\n    77→    /// is not desired.\n    78→    #[inline]\n    79→    pub fn stop(self) -> Duration {\n    80→        Instant::now() - self.started\n    81→    }\n    82→}\n    83→\n    84→impl<'a> Drop for ScopedTimer<'a> {\n    85→    fn drop(&mut self) {\n    86→        let elapsed = Instant::now() - self.started;\n    87→        info!(\n    88→            label = %self.label,\n    89→            ms = %elapsed.as_millis(),\n    90→            \"ScopedTimer finished\"\n    91→        );\n    92→        METRICS\n    93→            .write()\n    94→            .record_duration(self.label, elapsed.as_secs_f64());\n    95→    }\n    96→}\n    97→\n    98→/// Global metrics collector with *zero* external dependencies.  This is\n    99→/// intentionally minimal because higher layers might choose to forward these\n   100→/// numbers to Prometheus, StatsD, or another monitoring backend.\n   101→///\n   102→/// Internally it stores *exponential moving averages* so that it can be used\n   103→/// in hot-paths without unbounded memory growth.\n   104→pub struct Metrics {\n   105→    /// Moving average of durations (seconds) per label.\n   106→    durations: std::collections::HashMap<String, f64>,\n   107→    /// EWMA smoothing factor in `[0,1]`.\n   108→    alpha: f64,\n   109→}\n   110→\n   111→impl Metrics {\n   112→    fn new(alpha: f64) -> Self {\n   113→        Self {\n   114→            durations: std::collections::HashMap::new(),\n   115→            alpha,\n   116→        }\n   117→    }\n   118→\n   119→    /// Record a latency sample under `label`.\n   120→    pub fn record_duration(&mut self, label: impl Into<String>, secs: f64) {\n   121→        let label = label.into();\n   122→        let entry = self.durations.entry(label).or_insert(secs);\n   123→        // EWMA: v_t = alpha * x_t + (1-alpha) * v_{t-1}\n   124→        *entry = self.alpha * secs + (1.0 - self.alpha) * *entry;\n   125→    }\n   126→\n   127→    /// Return the current EWMA for `label`, if any.\n   128→    pub fn duration_avg(&self, label: &str) -> Option<f64> {\n   129→        self.durations.get(label).copied()\n   130→    }\n   131→}\n   132→\n   133→/// Static metrics instance; tweak the smoothing factor as needed.\n   134→/// We protect it with a *fair* RwLock to allow many concurrent readers.\n   135→static METRICS: Lazy<RwLock<Metrics>> = Lazy::new(|| RwLock::new(Metrics::new(0.20)));\n   136→\n   137→/// Typed representation of the global **application config**.\n   138→///\n   139→/// This is intentionally limited to fields that the utility layer needs\n   140→/// to know about; higher layers maintain their own extensions.\n   141→///\n   142→/// Because config files are versioned in the Model Registry, we include a\n   143→/// `schema_version` field to prevent silent breakage when the expected\n   144→/// structure changes.\n   145→#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n   146→pub struct AppConfig {\n   147→    pub schema_version: u16,\n   148→    pub experiment_tracking_uri: Option<String>,\n   149→    pub model_registry_uri: Option<String>,\n   150→    pub default_retry: RetryConfig,\n   151→    /// Additional arbitrary key/value flavour that can be stored alongside\n   152→    /// the canonical config.  Can be abused for feature flags, etc.\n   153→    #[serde(default)]\n   154→    pub user: std::collections::HashMap<String, serde_json::Value>,\n   155→}\n   156→\n   157→#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n   158→pub struct RetryConfig {\n   159→    pub max_attempts: usize,\n   160→    pub backoff_millis: u64,\n   161→}\n   162→\n   163→impl Default for RetryConfig {\n   164→    fn default() -> Self {\n   165→        Self {\n   166→            max_attempts: 3,\n   167→            backoff_millis: 500,\n   168→        }\n   169→    }\n   170→}\n   171→\n   172→/// Maximum size (bytes) that we allow a configuration file to have before\n   173→/// we refuse to load it.  Protects against accidental loading of huge blobs.\n   174→const MAX_CONFIG_SIZE: usize = 256 * 1024; // 256 KiB\n   175→\n   176→/// Load and parse application configuration **once** at start-up.\n   177→///\n   178→/// The file format is auto-detected based on extension (`.yaml`/`.yml` or\n   179→/// `.json`).  The resulting struct is cached in a global `Lazy`.\n   180→///\n   181→/// Example:\n   182→/// ```no_run\n   183→/// let cfg = visu_tility_utils::config();\n   184→/// println!(\"Schema v{} loaded\", cfg.schema_version);\n   185→/// ```\n   186→fn read_config_from_disk(path: &Path) -> Result<AppConfig> {\n   187→    if !path.exists() {\n   188→        return Err(UtilError::ConfigNotFound(path.into()).into());\n   189→    }\n   190→\n   191→    let mut file =\n   192→        File::open(path).with_context(|| format!(\"opening configuration file `{}`\", path.display()))?;\n   193→\n   194→    let mut buf = Vec::new();\n   195→    file.read_to_end(&mut buf)\n   196→        .with_context(|| format!(\"reading configuration file `{}`\", path.display()))?;\n   197→\n   198→    if buf.len() > MAX_CONFIG_SIZE {\n   199→        return Err(anyhow!(\n   200→            \"configuration file `{}` larger than {} KiB\",\n   201→            path.display(),\n   202→            MAX_CONFIG_SIZE / 1024\n   203→        ));\n   204→    }\n   205→\n   206→    let cfg = match path.extension().and_then(|s| s.to_str()) {\n   207→        Some(\"yaml\") | Some(\"yml\") => serde_yaml::from_slice(&buf)?,\n   208→        Some(\"json\") => serde_json::from_slice(&buf)?,\n   209→        other => {\n   210→            return Err(anyhow!(\n   211→                \"unsupported configuration extension {:?} for `{}`\",\n   212→                other,\n   213→                path.display()\n   214→            ))\n   215→        }\n   216→    };\n   217→\n   218→    Ok(cfg)\n   219→}\n   220→\n   221→/// Discover the config path in the following order:\n   222→///\n   223→/// 1. Explicit argument via `VISU_CONFIG` environment variable.\n   224→/// 2. `${CWD}/visu_config.yaml`\n   225→/// 3. `${CWD}/visu_config.json`\n   226→fn auto_discover_config() -> Option<PathBuf> {\n   227→    if let Ok(path) = std::env::var(\"VISU_CONFIG\") {\n   228→        return Some(PathBuf::from(path));\n   229→    }\n   230→\n   231→    const CANDIDATES: &[&str] = &[\"visu_config.yaml\", \"visu_config.yml\", \"visu_config.json\"];\n   232→    for c in CANDIDATES {\n   233→        let p = PathBuf::from(c);\n   234→        if p.exists() {\n   235→            return Some(p);\n   236→        }\n   237→    }\n   238→    None\n   239→}\n   240→\n   241→/// Global application configuration singleton.\n   242→static CONFIG: Lazy<AppConfig> = Lazy::new(|| {\n   243→    let path = auto_discover_config()\n   244→        .unwrap_or_else(|| PathBuf::from(\"visu_config.yaml\")); // fallback for nicer error msg\n   245→    match read_config_from_disk(&path) {\n   246→        Ok(cfg) => {\n   247→            info!(\n   248→                file = %path.display(),\n   249→                version = cfg.schema_version,\n   250→                \"configuration file loaded\"\n   251→            );\n   252→            cfg\n   253→        }\n   254→        Err(e) => {\n   255→            error!(\n   256→                file = %path.display(),\n   257→                error = %e,\n   258→                \"failed to load configuration; using built-in defaults\"\n   259→            );\n   260→            // Fallback\n   261→            AppConfig {\n   262→                schema_version: 0,\n   263→                experiment_tracking_uri: None,\n   264→                model_registry_uri: None,\n   265→                default_retry: RetryConfig::default(),\n   266→                user: Default::default(),\n   267→            }\n   268→        }\n   269→    }\n   270→});\n   271→\n   272→/// Access the application-wide [`AppConfig`].\n   273→#[inline]\n   274→pub fn config() -> &'static AppConfig {\n   275→    &CONFIG\n   276→}\n   277→\n   278→/// Generic exponential *back-off* / *retry* helper.\n   279→///\n   280→/// The provided closure is executed until it either succeeds or\n   281→/// max_attempts have been reached.\n   282→///\n   283→/// Usage:\n   284→/// ```ignore\n   285→/// use visu_tility_utils::retry;\n   286→///\n   287→/// let result = retry(\n   288→///     &config().default_retry,\n   289→///     || {\n   290→///         // call external dependency that might fail\n   291→///         some_unstable_operation()\n   292→///     });\n   293→/// ```\n   294→pub fn retry<F, T, E>(cfg: &RetryConfig, mut f: F) -> Result<T, E>\n   295→where\n   296→    F: FnMut() -> Result<T, E>,\n   297→{\n   298→    let mut attempt = 0;\n   299→    loop {\n   300→        match f() {\n   301→            Ok(v) => return Ok(v),\n   302→            Err(e) if attempt + 1 == cfg.max_attempts => return Err(e),\n   303→            Err(_e) => {\n   304→                attempt += 1;\n   305→                std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(cfg.backoff_millis));\n   306→            }\n   307→        }\n   308→    }\n   309→}\n   310→\n   311→/// Simple **ID generator** that hands out monotonically increasing\n   312→/// identifiers from a global counter.\n   313→///\n   314→/// This is useful when datasets or intermediate artefacts need a\n   315→/// transaction-like handle but heavyweight UUIDs are overkill.\n   316→#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]\n   317→pub struct Id(u64);\n   318→\n   319→// global counter\n   320→static COUNTER: AtomicUsize = AtomicUsize::new(1);\n   321→\n   322→impl Id {\n   323→    /// Allocate a fresh id.\n   324→    #[inline]\n   325→    pub fn fresh() -> Self {\n   326→        let n = COUNTER.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);\n   327→        Id(n as u64)\n   328→    }\n   329→\n   330→    /// Return the underlying integer.\n   331→    #[inline]\n   332→    pub fn as_u64(self) -> u64 {\n   333→        self.0\n   334→    }\n   335→}\n   336→\n   337→impl fmt::Display for Id {\n   338→    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {\n   339→        self.0.fmt(f)\n   340→    }\n   341→}\n   342→\n   343→/// **Observer pattern**: runtime event bus.\n   344→//\n   345→/// Very light-weight publisher/subscriber abstraction built on top of\n   346→/// [`crossbeam_channel`].  Since layers of the Orchestrator run in\n   347→/// different scheduling contexts we cannot rely on *any* runtime; a\n   348→/// channel-based design has zero dependencies on async executors.\n   349→pub mod event_bus {\n   350→    use super::*;\n   351→    use crossbeam_channel::{unbounded, Receiver, Sender};\n   352→\n   353→    /// Event types that travel on the bus. Generic utilities commonly used across VisuTility Orchestrator.\n//!\n//! The abstractions in this module are intentionally framework-agnostic:\n//! they do not depend on any particular CV/ML crate and can therefore be\n//! shared by every layer (Ingress, Data-Ops, Feature-Ops, …).\n//!\n//! All functions are `#[inline]` where it makes sense to do so, and\n//! significant branches are annotated with `#[cold]` to let the optimizer\n//! keep hot-paths small.\n\nuse std::{\n    fmt,\n    fs::File,\n    io::Read,\n    path::{Path, PathBuf},\n    sync::atomic::{AtomicUsize, Ordering},\n    time::{Duration, Instant},\n};\n\nuse anyhow::{anyhow, Context, Result};\nuse once_cell::sync::Lazy;\nuse parking_lot::RwLock;\nuse serde::de::DeserializeOwned;\nuse serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};\nuse thiserror::Error;\nuse tracing::{debug, error, info, instrument};\n\n/// VisuTility's generic error type for utility-layer operations.\n///\n/// We prefer using [`anyhow::Error`] at the top-level application\n/// boundary to allow for heterogeneous sources, but within the\n/// utility layer we capture the most common failure categories so that\n/// callers can still pattern-match when they need to.\n#[derive(Debug, Error)]\npub enum UtilError {\n    #[error(\"I/O error: {0}\")]\n    Io(#[from] std::io::Error),\n\n    #[error(\"serialization error: {0}\")]\n    Serde(#[from] serde_json::Error),\n\n    #[error(\"configuration not found at path `{0}`\")]\n    ConfigNotFound(PathBuf),\n\n    #[error(\"task failed: {0}\")]\n    TaskFailed(String),\n}\n\n/// Generic wrapper around an [`Instant`] which logs the elapsed time at\n/// **drop**.  The measurement is therefore *scoped*; as soon as the guard\n/// goes out of scope, latency is emitted through the `tracing` facade.\n///\n/// ```no_run\n/// use visu_tility_utils::ScopedTimer;\n/// let _g = ScopedTimer::new(\"heavy_initialisation\");\n/// // … some expensive code …\n— guard drops here and we get a log line with\n//   the elapsed time\n/// ```\npub struct ScopedTimer<'a> {\n    started: Instant,\n    label: &'a str,\n}\n\nimpl<'a> ScopedTimer<'a> {\n    /// Begin measuring elapsed time for the supplied label.\n    #[inline]\n    pub fn new(label: &'a str) -> Self {\n        Self {\n            started: Instant::now(),\n            label,\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Stop the timer and return the [`Duration`] since creation without\n    /// logging. This allows manual control when automatic logging at `drop`\n    /// is not desired.\n    #[inline]\n    pub fn stop(self) -> Duration {\n        Instant::now() - self.started\n    }\n}\n\nimpl<'a> Drop for ScopedTimer<'a> {\n    fn drop(&mut self) {\n        let elapsed = Instant::now() - self.started;\n        info!(\n            label = %self.label,\n            ms = %elapsed.as_millis(),\n            \"ScopedTimer finished\"\n        );\n        METRICS\n            .write()\n            .record_duration(self.label, elapsed.as_secs_f64());\n    }\n}\n\n/// Global metrics collector with *zero* external dependencies.  This is\n/// intentionally minimal because higher layers might choose to forward these\n/// numbers to Prometheus, StatsD, or another monitoring backend.\n///\n/// Internally it stores *exponential moving averages* so that it can be used\n/// in hot-paths without unbounded memory growth.\npub struct Metrics {\n    /// Moving average of durations (seconds) per label.\n    durations: std::collections::HashMap<String, f64>,\n    /// EWMA smoothing factor in `[0,1]`.\n    alpha: f64,\n}\n\nimpl Metrics {\n    fn new(alpha: f64) -> Self {\n        Self {\n            durations: std::collections::HashMap::new(),\n            alpha,\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Record a latency sample under `label`.\n    pub fn record_duration(&mut self, label: impl Into<String>, secs: f64) {\n        let label = label.into();\n        let entry = self.durations.entry(label).or_insert(secs);\n        // EWMA: v_t = alpha * x_t + (1-alpha) * v_{t-1}\n        *entry = self.alpha * secs + (1.0 - self.alpha) * *entry;\n    }\n\n    /// Return the current EWMA for `label`, if any.\n    pub fn duration_avg(&self, label: &str) -> Option<f64> {\n        self.durations.get(label).copied()\n    }\n}\n\n/// Static metrics instance; tweak the smoothing factor as needed.\n/// We protect it with a *fair* RwLock to allow many concurrent readers.\nstatic METRICS: Lazy<RwLock<Metrics>> = Lazy::new(|| RwLock::new(Metrics::new(0.20)));\n\n/// Typed representation of the global **application config**.\n///\n/// This is intentionally limited to fields that the utility layer needs\n/// to know about; higher layers maintain their own extensions.\n///\n/// Because config files are versioned in the Model Registry, we include a\n/// `schema_version` field to prevent silent breakage when the expected\n/// structure changes.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct AppConfig {\n    pub schema_version: u16,\n    pub experiment_tracking_uri: Option<String>,\n    pub model_registry_uri: Option<String>,\n    pub default_retry: RetryConfig,\n    /// Additional arbitrary key/value flavour that can be stored alongside\n    /// the canonical config.  Can be abused for feature flags, etc.\n    #[serde(default)]\n    pub user: std::collections::HashMap<String, serde_json::Value>,\n}\n\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct RetryConfig {\n    pub max_attempts: usize,\n    pub backoff_millis: u64,\n}\n\nimpl Default for RetryConfig {\n    fn default() -> Self {\n        Self {\n            max_attempts: 3,\n            backoff_millis: 500,\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Maximum size (bytes) that we allow a configuration file to have before\n/// we refuse to load it.  Protects against accidental loading of huge blobs.\nconst MAX_CONFIG_SIZE: usize = 256 * 1024; // 256 KiB\n\n/// Load and parse application configuration **once** at start-up.\n///\n/// The file format is auto-detected based on extension (`.yaml`/`.yml` or\n/// `.json`).  The resulting struct is cached in a global `Lazy`.\n///\n/// Example:\n/// ```no_run\n/// let cfg = visu_tility_utils::config();\n/// println!(\"Schema v{} loaded\", cfg.schema_version);\n/// ```\nfn read_config_from_disk(path: &Path) -> Result<AppConfig> {\n    if !path.exists() {\n        return Err(UtilError::ConfigNotFound(path.into()).into());\n    }\n\n    let mut file =\n        File::open(path).with_context(|| format!(\"opening configuration file `{}`\", path.display()))?;\n\n    let mut buf = Vec::new();\n    file.read_to_end(&mut buf)\n        .with_context(|| format!(\"reading configuration file `{}`\", path.display()))?;\n\n    if buf.len() > MAX_CONFIG_SIZE {\n        return Err(anyhow!(\n            \"configuration file `{}` larger than {} KiB\",\n            path.display(),\n            MAX_CONFIG_SIZE / 1024\n        ));\n    }\n\n    let cfg = match path.extension().and_then(|s| s.to_str()) {\n        Some(\"yaml\") | Some(\"yml\") => serde_yaml::from_slice(&buf)?,\n        Some(\"json\") => serde_json::from_slice(&buf)?,\n        other => {\n            return Err(anyhow!(\n                \"unsupported configuration extension {:?} for `{}`\",\n                other,\n                path.display()\n            ))\n        }\n    };\n\n    Ok(cfg)\n}\n\n/// Discover the config path in the following order:\n///\n/// 1. Explicit argument via `VISU_CONFIG` environment variable.\n/// 2. `${CWD}/visu_config.yaml`\n/// 3. `${CWD}/visu_config.json`\nfn auto_discover_config() -> Option<PathBuf> {\n    if let Ok(path) = std::env::var(\"VISU_CONFIG\") {\n        return Some(PathBuf::from(path));\n    }\n\n    const CANDIDATES: &[&str] = &[\"visu_config.yaml\", \"visu_config.yml\", \"visu_config.json\"];\n    for c in CANDIDATES {\n        let p = PathBuf::from(c);\n        if p.exists() {\n            return Some(p);\n        }\n    }\n    None\n}\n\n/// Global application configuration singleton.\nstatic CONFIG: Lazy<AppConfig> = Lazy::new(|| {\n    let path = auto_discover_config()\n        .unwrap_or_else(|| PathBuf::from(\"visu_config.yaml\")); // fallback for nicer error msg\n    match read_config_from_disk(&path) {\n        Ok(cfg) => {\n            info!(\n                file = %path.display(),\n                version = cfg.schema_version,\n                \"configuration file loaded\"\n            );\n            cfg\n        }\n        Err(e) => {\n            error!(\n                file = %path.display(),\n                error = %e,\n                \"failed to load configuration; using built-in defaults\"\n            );\n            // Fallback\n            AppConfig {\n                schema_version: 0,\n                experiment_tracking_uri: None,\n                model_registry_uri: None,\n                default_retry: RetryConfig::default(),\n                user: Default::default(),\n            }\n        }\n    }\n});\n\n/// Access the application-wide [`AppConfig`].\n#[inline]\npub fn config() -> &'static AppConfig {\n    &CONFIG\n}\n\n/// Generic exponential *back-off* / *retry* helper.\n///\n/// The provided closure is executed until it either succeeds or\n/// max_attempts have been reached.\n///\n/// Usage:\n/// ```ignore\n/// use visu_tility_utils::retry;\n///\n/// let result = retry(\n///     &config().default_retry,\n///     || {\n///         // call external dependency that might fail\n///         some_unstable_operation()\n///     });\n/// ```\npub fn retry<F, T, E>(cfg: &RetryConfig, mut f: F) -> Result<T, E>\nwhere\n    F: FnMut() -> Result<T, E>,\n{\n    let mut attempt = 0;\n    loop {\n        match f() {\n            Ok(v) => return Ok(v),\n            Err(e) if attempt + 1 == cfg.max_attempts => return Err(e),\n            Err(_e) => {\n                attempt += 1;\n                std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(cfg.backoff_millis));\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Simple **ID generator** that hands out monotonically increasing\n/// identifiers from a global counter.\n///\n/// This is useful when datasets or intermediate artefacts need a\n/// transaction-like handle but heavyweight UUIDs are overkill.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]\npub struct Id(u64);\n\n// global counter\nstatic COUNTER: AtomicUsize = AtomicUsize::new(1);\n\nimpl Id {\n    /// Allocate a fresh id.\n    #[inline]\n    pub fn fresh() -> Self {\n        let n = COUNTER.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);\n        Id(n as u64)\n    }\n\n    /// Return the underlying integer.\n    #[inline]\n    pub fn as_u64(self) -> u64 {\n        self.0\n    }\n}\n\nimpl fmt::Display for Id {\n    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {\n        self.0.fmt(f)\n    }\n}\n\n/// **Observer pattern**: runtime event bus.\n//\n/// Very light-weight publisher/subscriber abstraction built on top of\n/// [`crossbeam_channel`].  Since layers of the Orchestrator run in\n/// different scheduling contexts we cannot rely on *any* runtime; a\n/// channel-based design has zero dependencies on async executors.\npub mod event_bus {\n    use super::*;\n    use crossbeam_channel::{unbounded, Receiver, Sender};\n\n    /// Event types that travel on the bus.  This enum grows over time as new\n    /// features make use of the bus; by keeping it located in a *utility*\n    /// crate we avoid circular dependencies.\n    #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n    pub enum Event {\n        HyperparameterTuningStarted { experiment_id: Id },\n        HyperparameterTuningFinished { experiment_id: Id },\n        ModelRegistered {\n            name: String,\n            version: String,\n            metadata: std::collections::HashMap<String, String>,\n        },\n        ModelDriftDetected {\n            model: String,\n            magnitude: f32,\n            timestamp: chrono::DateTime<chrono::Utc>,\n        },\n        Custom(String, serde_json::Value),\n    }\n\n    /// Handle required for publishing events.\n    #[derive(Clone)]\n    pub struct Publisher {\n        tx: Sender<Event>,\n    }\n\n    impl Publisher {\n        /// Publish an event; errors are logged but otherwise ignored since the\n        /// system should remain functional even when no-one is listening.\n        pub fn publish(&self, evt: Event) {\n            if let Err(e) = self.tx.send(evt.clone()) {\n                debug!(error = ?e, event = ?evt, \"failed to publish event\");\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// A stream of events.\n    pub type Subscription = Receiver<Event>;\n\n    /// Construct a **single** global bus so that layers can obtain\n    /// cheap clones of the `Publisher` without having to pass them around.\n    static BUS: Lazy<(Publisher, Subscription)> = Lazy::new(|| {\n        let (tx, rx) = unbounded::<Event>();\n        (Publisher { tx }, rx)\n    });\n\n    /// Return a handle for *publishing* events.\n    pub fn publisher() -> Publisher {\n        BUS.0.clone()\n    }\n\n    /// Subscribe to events.  Every caller receives a *clone* of the receiver\n    /// which means all subscribers see *all* events (fan-out).\n    pub fn subscribe() -> Subscription {\n        BUS.1.clone()\n    }\n}\n\n/// A small proc-macro-like helper (implemented as declarative macro to avoid meta\n/// crate) for scoped timing.\n///\n/// ```\n/// visu_tility_utils::scope_time!(\"fancy_stage\", {\n///     expensive();\n/// });\n/// ```\n#[macro_export]\nmacro_rules! scope_time {\n    ($label:expr, $block:block) => {{\n        let _g = $crate::ScopedTimer::new($label);\n        $block\n    }};\n}\n\n/// Tests live inside the same file so that the utils crate remains\n/// a **single** compilation unit.  This is important for global singletons\n/// (`Lazy`) which must not be duplicated across multiple crates.\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n\n    #[test]\n    fn id_monotonicity() {\n        let a = Id::fresh();\n        let b = Id::fresh();\n        assert!(b.as_u64() > a.as_u64());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn retry_succeeds_eventually() {\n        let cfg = RetryConfig {\n            max_attempts: 5,\n            backoff_millis: 1,\n        };\n        let mut n = 0;\n        let res: Result<usize, usize> = retry(&cfg, || {\n            if n < 3 {\n                n += 1;\n                Err(n)\n            } else {\n                Ok(n)\n            }\n        });\n        assert_eq!(res.unwrap(), 3);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn metrics_ewma() {\n        METRICS.write().record_duration(\"foo\", 1.0);\n        METRICS.write().record_duration(\"foo\", 1.0);\n        let avg = METRICS.read().duration_avg(\"foo\").unwrap();\n        assert!(avg > 0.0 && avg <= 1.0);\n    }\n}","numLines":466,"startLine":1,"totalLines":466}}}

Module 7 – Model-Monitoring & Automated-Retraining Orchestrator\n//! -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n//! This module wires together the Strategy + Observer patterns to:\n//!   1. Collect model-serving metrics from asynchronous sources\n//!   2. Detect drift / SLA violations through pluggable observers\n//!   3. Decide—via retraining strategy—whether automated retraining should start\n//!   4. Emit retraining jobs into an async work-queue for downstream processing\n//!\n//! The implementation is intentionally self-contained so it can be compiled\n//! standalone for unit-testing, yet is ready to be integrated into the wider\n//! `VisuTility Orchestrator` platform.\n//!\n//! External crates used:\n//!   - tokio      (async runtime + channels)\n//!   - anyhow     (error propagation)\n//!   - serde      (de/serialization of metric payloads)\n//!   - thiserror  (typed error handling)\n//!   - log        (structured + leveled logging)\n//!   - metrics    (project-wide metrics facade)\n//\n//! -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nuse std::{\n    collections::VecDeque,\n    sync::Arc,\n    time::{Duration, SystemTime},\n};\n\nuse anyhow::Result;\nuse log::{debug, error, info, warn};\nuse metrics::{counter, gauge};\nuse serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};\nuse thiserror::Error;\nuse tokio::{\n    select,\n    sync::{broadcast, mpsc, oneshot, RwLock},\n    task::JoinHandle,\n    time,\n};\n\n/// Maximum number of historical events kept in memory per model.\nconst MAX_WINDOW_SIZE: usize = 5_000;\n/// Channel size for metric ingestion.\nconst METRIC_CHANNEL_CAPACITY: usize = 2048;\n/// Channel size for retraining job queue.\nconst RETRAIN_CHANNEL_CAPACITY: usize = 128;\n/// Default evaluation interval (how often we evaluate retraining strategies).\nconst DEFAULT_EVALUATION_INTERVAL: Duration = Duration::from_secs(60);\n\n/// Error types specific to Monitoring.\n#[derive(Debug, Error)]\npub enum MonitorError {\n    #[error(\"metrics channel closed\")]\n    ChannelClosed,\n    #[error(\"failed to send retraining job: {0}\")]\n    RetrainSend(String),\n    #[error(\"internal error: {0}\")]\n    Internal(String),\n}\n\n/// Public handle that can be cloned and used by inference workers to\n/// asynchronously submit new metric events.\n#[derive(Clone)]\npub struct MetricSink {\n    tx: mpsc::Sender<MetricEvent>,\n}\n\nimpl MetricSink {\n    pub async fn submit(&self, evt: MetricEvent) -> Result<(), MonitorError> {\n        self.tx\n            .send(evt)\n            .await\n            .map_err(|_| MonitorError::ChannelClosed)\n    }\n}\n\n/// Public stream (subscriber) receiving retraining jobs that need handling\n/// downstream by Model-Ops components (e.g. a trainer dispatcher).\n#[derive(Clone)]\npub struct RetrainJobStream {\n    rx: broadcast::Receiver<RetrainJob>,\n}\n\nimpl RetrainJobStream {\n    pub async fn recv(&mut self) -> Result<RetrainJob, MonitorError> {\n        self.rx\n            .recv()\n            .await\n            .map_err(|e| MonitorError::RetrainSend(e.to_string()))\n    }\n}\n\n/// Metric payload emitted by serving-layer components.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct MetricEvent {\n    pub model_id: String,\n    /// Unix-timestamp in milliseconds.\n    pub ts: u128,\n    /// End-to-end request latency observed by the client (ms).\n    pub latency_ms: f64,\n    /// Instantaneous throughput (# predictions / sec)\n    pub throughput: f64,\n    /// Running accuracy estimate (0.0 – 1.0) if labels available.\n    pub accuracy: Option<f64>,\n}\n\nimpl MetricEvent {\n    pub fn now(\n        model_id: impl Into<String>,\n        latency_ms: f64,\n        throughput: f64,\n        accuracy: Option<f64>,\n    ) -> Self {\n        Self {\n            model_id: model_id.into(),\n            ts: SystemTime::now()\n                .duration_since(SystemTime::UNIX_EPOCH)\n                .expect(\"time went backwards\")\n                .as_millis(),\n            latency_ms,\n            throughput,\n            accuracy,\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Rolling collection of metric events for a single model.\n#[derive(Debug, Default)]\nstruct MetricWindow {\n    /// Time-ordered ring-buffer\n    events: VecDeque<MetricEvent>,\n    /// Pre-aggregated stats for constant-time observers\n    latency_sum: f64,\n    throughput_sum: f64,\n    accuracy_sum: f64,\n    accuracy_cnt: usize,\n}\n\nimpl MetricWindow {\n    fn push(&mut self, evt: MetricEvent) {\n        if self.events.len() == MAX_WINDOW_SIZE {\n            if let Some(old) = self.events.pop_front() {\n                self.latency_sum -= old.latency_ms;\n                self.throughput_sum -= old.throughput;\n                if let Some(acc) = old.accuracy {\n                    self.accuracy_sum -= acc;\n                    self.accuracy_cnt -= 1;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        self.latency_sum += evt.latency_ms;\n        self.throughput_sum += evt.throughput;\n        if let Some(acc) = evt.accuracy {\n            self.accuracy_sum += acc;\n            self.accuracy_cnt += 1;\n        }\n\n        self.events.push_back(evt);\n    }\n\n    fn len(&self) -> usize {\n        self.events.len()\n    }\n\n    fn mean_latency(&self) -> f64 {\n        if self.events.is_empty() {\n            return 0.0;\n        }\n        self.latency_sum / self.events.len() as f64\n    }\n\n    fn mean_throughput(&self) -> f64 {\n        if self.events.is_empty() {\n            return 0.0;\n        }\n        self.throughput_sum / self.events.len() as f64\n    }\n\n    fn mean_accuracy(&self) -> Option<f64> {\n        if self.accuracy_cnt == 0 {\n            None\n        } else {\n            Some(self.accuracy_sum / self.accuracy_cnt as f64)\n        }\n    }\n\n    fn p99_latency(&self) -> f64 {\n        // naive percentile calc (production code may use histogram)\n        if self.events.is_empty() {\n            return 0.0;\n        }\n        let mut latencies: Vec<_> = self.events.iter().map(|e| e.latency_ms).collect();\n        latencies.sort_by(|a, b| a.partial_cmp(b).unwrap());\n        let idx = ((latencies.len() as f64) * 0.99).ceil() as usize - 1;\n        latencies[idx]\n    }\n}\n\n/// What action an Observer suggests.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq)]\nenum MonitorAction {\n    Noop,\n    FlagRetrain,\n}\n\n/// Observer Pattern: notified of each model’s aggregated metrics.\ntrait MetricObserver: Send + Sync {\n    /// Name (for logging/metrics)\n    fn name(&self) -> &'static str;\n    /// Evaluate window; return action.\n    fn on_update(&self, model: &str, window: &MetricWindow) -> MonitorAction;\n}\n\n/// Observer that triggers retraining when P99 latency > threshold.\nstruct LatencySlaObserver {\n    /// milliseconds\n    threshold_ms: f64,\n}\n\nimpl LatencySlaObserver {\n    fn new(threshold_ms: f64) -> Self {\n        Self { threshold_ms }\n    }\n}\n\nimpl MetricObserver for LatencySlaObserver {\n    fn name(&self) -> &'static str {\n        \"latency_sla\"\n    }\n\n    fn on_update(&self, model: &str, window: &MetricWindow) -> MonitorAction {\n        let p99 = window.p99_latency();\n        gauge!(\"model.p99_latency\", p99, \"model\" => model);\n        if p99 > self.threshold_ms {\n            warn!(\n                \"Model {} P99 latency {:.2}ms above threshold {:.2}ms\",\n                model, p99, self.threshold_ms\n            );\n            return MonitorAction::FlagRetrain;\n        }\n        MonitorAction::Noop\n    }\n}\n\n/// Observer that detects accuracy drift below threshold.\nstruct AccuracyDriftObserver {\n    min_accuracy: f64,\n}\n\nimpl AccuracyDriftObserver {\n    fn new(min_accuracy: f64) -> Self {\n        Self { min_accuracy }\n    }\n}\n\nimpl MetricObserver for AccuracyDriftObserver {\n    fn name(&self) -> gauge!(\"model.accuracy\", acc, \"model\" => model);\n            if acc < self.min_accuracy {\n                warn!(\n                    \"Model {} accuracy {:.4} below threshold {:.4}\",\n                    model, acc, self.min_accuracy\n                );\n                return MonitorAction::FlagRetrain;\n            }\n        }\n        MonitorAction::Noop\n    }\n}\n\n/// An internal representation of a retraining job request.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct RetrainJob {\n    pub model_id: String,\n    pub reason: String,\n    pub requested_at: u128,\n}\n\n/// Strategy Pattern: decides—based on aggregated Observer signals—if we should\n/// create a RetrainJob.\n#[async_trait::async_trait]\ntrait RetrainStrategy: Send + Sync {\n    /// Called periodically with aggregated observer actions\n    async fn evaluate(\n        &self,\n        model: &str,\n        actions: &[MonitorAction],\n        window: &MetricWindow,\n    ) -> Option<RetrainJob>;\n}\n\n/// Simple majority-vote strategy: if any FlagRetrain action is present,\n/// we create a job. Implements a debounce window to avoid spamming.\nstruct MajorityStrategy {\n    /// minimum time between two jobs for the same model\n    cooldown: Duration,\n    /// book-keeping of last job timestamps\n    last_job_ts: Arc<RwLock<dashmap::DashMap<String, u128>>>,\n}\n\nimpl MajorityStrategy {\n    fn new(cooldown: Duration) -> Self {\n        Self {\n            cooldown,\n            last_job_ts: Arc::new(RwLock::new(dashmap::DashMap::new())),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n#[async_trait::async_trait]\nimpl RetrainStrategy for MajorityStrategy {\n    async fn evaluate(\n        &self,\n        model: &str,\n        actions: &[MonitorAction],\n        _window: &MetricWindow,\n    ) -> Option<RetrainJob> {\n        if !actions.iter().any(|a| *a == MonitorAction::FlagRetrain) {\n            return None;\n        }\n\n        let now = SystemTime::now()\n            .duration_since(SystemTime::UNIX_EPOCH)\n            .unwrap()\n            .as_millis();\n\n        {\n            let m = self.last_job_ts.read().await;\n            if let Some(entry) = m.get(model) {\n                let last = *entry;\n                if now - last < self.cooldown.as_millis() {\n                    debug!(\"Skipping retrain for {model} due to cooldown\");\n                    return None;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        {\n            let mut m = self.last_job_ts.write().await;\n            m.insert(model.to_string(), now);\n        }\n\n        Some(RetrainJob {\n            model_id: model.to_string(),\n            reason: \"observer_trigger\".into(),\n            requested_at: now,\n        })\n    }\n}\n\n/// Central engine wiring together observers + strategy.\npub struct MonitorEngine {\n    metric_sink: observers: Vec<Box<dyn MetricObserver>>,\n        strategy: Arc<dyn RetrainStrategy>,\n    ) -> Self {\n        let (metric_tx, mut metric_rx) = mpsc::channel::<MetricEvent>(METRIC_CHANNEL_CAPACITY);\n        let (retrain_tx, _) = broadcast::channel::<RetrainJob>(RETRAIN_CHANNEL_CAPACITY);\n\n        // In-memory state: per-model rolling window\n        let windows = Arc::new(RwLock::new(dashmap::DashMap::<String, MetricWindow>::new()));\n\n        // Task 1: metric ingestion\n        let windows_clone = Arc::clone(&windows);\n        let metric_task = tokio::spawn(async move {\n            while let Some(evt) = metric_rx.recv().await {\n                counter!(\"metric_events_ingested\", 1, \"model\" => &evt.model_id);\n\n                let mut windows = windows_clone.write().await;\n                let window = windows\n                    .entry(evt.model_id.clone())\n                    .or_insert_with(MetricWindow::default);\n                window.push(evt);\n            }\n        });\n\n        // Task 2: periodic evaluation\n        let evaluation_task = {\n            let windows = Arc::clone(&windows);\n            let observers = observers\n                .into_iter()\n                .map(|o| (o.name(), o))\n                .collect::<Vec<_>>();\n            let mut retrain_tx = retrain_tx.clone();\n\n            tokio::spawn(async move {\n                let mut ticker = time::interval(DEFAULT_EVALUATION_INTERVAL);\n                loop {\n                    ticker.tick().await;\n                    let windows_guard = windows.read().await;\n\n                    for entry in windows_guard.iter() {\n                        let model = entry.key();\n                        let window = entry.value();\n\n                        if window.len() < 100 {\n                            // Not enough data yet\n                            continue;\n                        }\n\n                        let mut actions = Vec::with_capacity(observers.len());\n                        for (_, obs) in &observers {\n                            let a = obs.on_update(model, window);\n                            actions.push(a);\n                        }\n\n                        // Evaluate strategy\n                        if let Some(job) =\n                            strategy.evaluate(model, &actions, window).await\n                        {\n                            match retrain_tx.send(job.clone()) {\n                                Ok(_) => {\n                                    info!(\"Enqueued retrain job for model {}\", model);\n                                    counter!(\"retrain_jobs_enqueued\", 1, \"model\" => model);\n                                }\n                                Err(e) => {\n                                    error!(\"Failed to enqueue retrain job: {}\", e);\n                                }\n                            }\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            })\n        };\n\n        Self {\n            metric_sink: MetricSink { tx: metric_tx },\n            retrain_job_stream: RetrainJobStream {\n                rx: retrain_tx.subscribe(),\n            },\n            tasks: vec![metric_task, evaluation_task],\n        }\n    }\n\n    pub fn sink(&self) -> MetricSink {\n        self.metric_sink.clone()\n    }\n\n    pub fn retrain_jobs(&self) -> RetrainJobStream {\n        self.retrain_job_stream.clone()\n    }\n\n    /// Gracefully stop tasks (await on JoinHandles)\n    pub async fn shutdown(self) {\n        for handle in self.tasks {\n            handle.abort(); // For brevity; in prod, send shutdown signal\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Example integration test (can be toggled with `cargo test --lib`)\n//     // Strategy\n        let strategy = Arc::new(MajorityStrategy::new(Duration::from_secs(30)));\n\n        // Start engine\n        let engine = MonitorEngine::start(observers, strategy);\n\n          MetricDirection::Minimize => self.value < other.value,\n    68→        }\n    69→    }\n    70→}\n    71→\n    72→/// Optimization direction for a metric.\n    73→#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, This module implements the Strategy + Observer patterns for\n//! hyper-parameter tuning.  Tuning strategies (`GridSearchTuner`,\n//! `RandomSearchTuner`, …) implement the [`HyperParamTuner`] trait,\n//! while interested parties subscribe to an async broadcast bus to\n//! receive fine-grained tuning events in real time.\n//!\n//! The code is self-contained and can be compiled independently, yet\n//! integrates cleanly into the VisuTility Orchestrator ecosystem:\n//!   • Uses `async_trait` for async polymorphism\n//!   • Emits structured logs via `tracing`\n//!   • Relies on `serde_json::Value` for schema-less parameter configs\n//!   • Provides unit tests powered by `tokio` runtime\n//!\n//! External crates required (add to Cargo.toml):\n//! ```toml\n//! async-trait   = \"0.1\"\n//! futures       = \"0.3\"\n//! maplit        = \"1.0\"\n//! rand          = \"0.8\"\n//! serde         = { version = \"1\", features = [\"derive\"] }\n//! serde_json    = \"1\"\n//! thiserror     = \"1\"\n//! tokio         = { version = \"1\", features = [\"full\"] }\n//! tracing       = \"0.1\"\n//! thiserror::Error;\nuse tokio::sync::broadcast;\nuse tokio::time::{timeout, Duration};\nuse tracing::{debug, error, info, instrument, warn};\n\n/// Generic, schema-less hyper-parameter map.\n///\n/// NOTE: In highly regulated environments you may want a stricter,\n/// schema-validated representation.\npub type HyperParams = HashMap<String, Value>;\n\n/// A single evaluation metric produced by a model run.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct Metric {\n    pub name: String,\n    pub value: f64,\n    pub direction: MetricDirection,\n}\n\nimpl Metric {\n    /// Returns `true` if `self` is better than `other` w.r.t. the\n    /// optimization direction.\n    pub fn is_better_than(&self, other: &Self) -> bool {\n        match self.direction {\n            MetricDirection::Maximize => self.value > other.value,\n            MetricDirection::Minimize => self.value < other.value,\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Optimization direction for a metric.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub enum MetricDirection {\n    Maximize,\n    Minimize,\n}\n\nimpl Display for MetricDirection {\n    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {\n        match self {\n            MetricDirection::Maximize => write!(f, \"maximize\"),\n            MetricDirection::Minimize => write!(f, \"minimize\"),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Result returned by any tuner.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct TuningResult {\n    pub best_params: HyperParams,\n    pub best_metric: Metric,\n}\n\n/// Domain-specific errors during tuning.\n#[derive(Error, Debug)]\npub enum TuningError {\n    #[error(\"model training failed: {0}\")]\n    TrainingFailure(String),\n\n    #[error(\"evaluation timed out\")]\n    EvalTimeout,\n\n    #[error(\"search space exhausted without receiving any metric\")]\n    EmptySearchSpace,\n\n    #[error(\"internal error: {0}\")]\n    Internal(String),\n}\n\nimpl TuningError {\n    pub fn internal<E: std::error::Error>(err: E) -> Self {\n        Self::Internal(err.to_string())\n    }\n}\n\n/// Minimal interface for something able to train + evaluate a model.\n#[async_trait]\npub trait ModelTrainer: Send + Sync {\n    async fn train_and_eval(&self, params: &HyperParams) -> Result<Metric, TuningError>;\n}\n\n/// Observer interface (Observer pattern).\n#[async_trait]\npub trait TuningEventObserver: Send + Sync {\n    async fn on_trial_start(&self, trial_id: usize, params: &HyperParams);\n    async fn on_trial_complete(\n        &self,\n        trial_id: usize,\n        params: &HyperParams,\n        metric: &Result<Metric, TuningError>,\n    );\n    async fn on_tuning_complete(&self, result: &Result<TuningResult, TuningError>);\n}\n\n/// Internal enumeration of broadcast events.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub enum TuningEvent {\n    TrialStart {\n        trial_id: usize,\n        params: HyperParams,\n    },\n    TrialComplete {\n        trial_id: usize,\n        params: HyperParams,\n        metric: Result<Metric, TuningError>,\n    },\n    TuningComplete {\n        result: Result<TuningResult, TuningError>,\n    },\n}\n\n/// Broadcast bus wrapping `tokio::sync::broadcast`.\n#[derive(Clone)]\npub struct TuningEventBus {\n    tx: broadcast::Sender<TuningEvent>,\n}\n\nimpl TuningEventBus {\n    pub fn new(buffer: usize) -> Self {\n        let (tx, _) = broadcast::channel(buffer);\n        Self { tx }\n    }\n\n    pub fn subscribe(&self) -> broadcast::Receiver<TuningEvent> {\n        self.tx.subscribe()\n    }\n\n    pub fn emit(&self, e: TuningEvent) {\n        // Ignoring send errors (occurs if no receiver is listening).\n        let _ = self.tx.send(e);\n    }\n}\n\n/// Trait that every tuning strategy must implement.\n#[async_trait]\npub trait HyperParamTuner: Send + Sync {\n    async fn tune(&self) -> Result<TuningResult, TuningError>;\n}\n\n/// Shared context passed to ────────────────────────────────\n//\n\n/// Exhaustive grid-search implementation.\npub struct GridSearchTuner<T: ModelTrainer> {\n    context: TunerContext<T>,\n    search_space: max_duration: Option<Duration>,\n    ) -> Self {\n        Self {\n            context: TunerContext {\n                trainer,\n                event_bus: TuningEventBus::new(event_buffer),\n                max_duration,\n            },\n            search_space,\n        }\n    }\n\n    pub fn event_bus(&self) -> TuningEventBus {\n        self.context.event_bus.clone()\n    }\n}\n\n#[async_trait]\nimpl<T> HyperParamTuner for GridSearchTuner<T>\nwhere\n    T: ModelTrainer + 'static,\n{\n    #[instrument(skip_all, name = \"grid_search_tune\")]\n    async fn tune(&self) -> Result<TuningResult, TuningError> {\n        let ctx = &self.context;\n\n        if self.search_space.is_empty() {\n            return Err(TuningError::EmptySearchSpace);\n        }\n\n        let mut best_metric: Option<Metric> = None;\n        let mut best_params: Option<HyperParams> = None;\n\n        let mut trials = stream::iter(self.search_space.clone().into_iter().enumerate());\n\n        while let Some((trial_id, params)) = trials.next().await {\n            ctx.event_bus.emit(TuningEvent::TrialStart {\n                trial_id,\n                params: params.clone(),\n            });\n\n            let metric_res = match ctx.max_duration {\n                Some(dur) => match timeout(dur, ctx.trainer.train_and_eval(&params)).await {\n                    Ok(res) => res,\n                    Err(_) => Err(TuningError::EvalTimeout),\n                },\n                None => ctx.trainer.train_and_eval(&params).await,\n            };\n\n            ctx.event_bus.emit(TuningEvent::TrialComplete {\n                trial_id,\n                params: params.clone(),\n                metric: metric_res.clone(),\n            });\n\n            match &metric_res {\n                Ok(metric) => {\n                    let better = best_metric\n                        .as_ref()\n                        .map(|m| metric.is_better_than(m))\n                        .unwrap_or(true);\n                    if better {\n                        best_metric = Some(metric.clone());\n                        best_params = Some(params);\n                    }\n                }\n                Err(e) => warn!(trial_id, error = %e, \"grid-search trial failed\"),\n            }\n        }\n\n        let result = match (best_params, best_metric) {\n \"all grid-search trials failed\".into(),\n            )),\n        };\n\n        ctx.event_bus\n            .emit(TuningEvent::TuningComplete { result: result.clone() });\n\n        result\n    }\n}\n\n//\n// ────────────────────────────────\n//  _____                 _\n/// |  __ \\               | |\n// | |__) |___  __ _  ___ | |_\n// |  _  // _ \\/ _` |/ _ \\| __|\n// | | \\ \\  __/ (_| | (_) | |_\n// |_|  \\_\\___|\\__, |\\___/ \\__|\n//              __/ |\n//             |___/\n// ────────────────────────────────\n//\n\n/// Random-search implementation.\npub struct               trial_id,\n                params: params.clone(),\n                metric: metric_res.clone(),\n            });\n\n            match &metric_res {\n                Ok(metric) => {\n }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Attach an observer to the bus; returns a detach-able async task.\npub fn attach_observer<O: TuningEventObserver + 'static>(\n    bus: &TuningEventBus,\n    observer: Arc<O>,\n) -> tokio::task::JoinHandle<()> {\n    let mut rx = bus.subscribe();\n    tokio::spawn(async move {\n        while let Ok(event) = rx.recv().await {\n            match event {\n All writes are versioned and immutable.\n#[derive(Debug, Default, Clone)]\npub struct ModelRegistry {\n    inner: Arc<RwLock<HashMap<ModelId, Vec<ModelVersion>>>>,\n}\n\nimpl ModelRegistry {\n    /// Registers a new model version and returns the assigned version number.\n    pub fn register(\n        &self,\n        id: &ModelId,\n        hyper_params: HyperParams,\n        metrics: Metrics,\n    ) -> Result<u64, ModelOpsError> {\n        let mut guard = self\n            .inner\n            .write()\n            .map_err(|e| ModelOpsError::Registry(e.to_string()))?;\n\n        let versions = guard.entry(id.clone()).or_default();\n        let version_num = versions\n            .last()\n            .map(|v| v.version + 1)\n            .unwrap_or(1);\n\n        versions.push(ModelVersion {\n            version: version_num,\n            trained_at: SystemTime::now(),\n            hyper_params,\n            metrics,\n        });\n\n        info!(\"Registered model `{id}` version {version_num}\");\n        Ok(version_num)\n    }\n\n    /// Returns the latest model version.\n    pub fn latest(&self, id: &ModelId) -> Result<ModelVersion, ModelOpsError> {\n        let guard = self\n            .inner\n            .read()\n            .map_err(|e| ModelOpsError::Registry(e.to_string()))?;\n\n        guard\n            .get(id)\n            .and_then(|v| v.last().cloned())\n            .ok_or_else(|| ModelOpsError::ModelNotFound(id.clone()))\n    }\n}\n\n//\n// Strategy Pattern: Hyper-Parameter Tuners\n// ---------------------------------------\n\n/// Strategies must &DataBatch,\n    ) -> Result<HyperParams, ModelOpsError>;\n}\n\n/// A simple grid search tuner.\npub struct GridSearchTuner {\n    learning_rates: Vec<f64>,\n    num_layers: Vec<f64>,\n}\n\nimpl }\n}\n\n#[async_trait]\nimpl HyperParamTuner for GridSearchTuner {\n    async fn tune(\n        &self,\n        model_id: &ModelId,\n        data: &DataBatch,\n    ) -> Result<HyperParams, ModelOpsError> {\n        info!(\n            \"Running grid search for model `{}` on {} samples\",\n            model_id, data.num_samples\n        );\n\n        // Dummy evaluation metric: random value ∈ [0,1]\n        let mut best_metric = f64::MIN;\n        let mut best_params = HyperParams::new();\n\n        for lr in &self.learning_rates {\n            for layers in &self.num_layers {\n                let simulated_metric = 1.0 / (lr * layers); // deterministic but arbitrary\n                if simulated_metric > best_metric {\n                    best_metric = simulated_metric;\n                    best_params.insert(\"learning_rate\".into(), *lr);\n                    best_params.insert(\"num_layers\".into(), *layers);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        info!(\"Grid search selected params: {:?}\", best_params);\n        Ok(best_params)\n    }\n}\n\n/// A random search tuner.\npub struct RandomSearchTuner {\n    trials: usize,\n    rng: ModelOpsError> {\n        info!(\n            \"Running random search for model `{}` with {} trials\",\n            model_id, self.trials\n        );\n\n        let lr_dist = Uniform::new(1e-4, 1e-2);\n        let layer_dist = Uniform::new_inclusive(2.0, 6.0);\n\n        let mut best_metric = f64::MIN;\n        let mut best_params = HyperParams::new();\n\n        for _ in 0..self.trials {\n .observers\n            .write()\n            .expect(\"poisoned lock in ModelMonitor\");\n        guard.push(observer);\n    }\n\n    async fn notify(&self, event: MetricsEvent) {\n        debug!(\"Notifying {} observers\", self.observers.read().unwrap().len());\n        for obs in self.observers.read().unwrap().iter() {\n            obs.update(event.clone()).await;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n//\n// Automated Retrainer\n// -------------------\n\n/// Retrains a model when metrics fall below a dynamic threshold.\npub struct AutoRetrainer {\n    registry: ModelRegistry,\n    tuner: degradation_threshold: f64,\n    ) -> Self {\n        Self {\n            registry,\n            tuner,\n            degradation_threshold,\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Actual training routine.  In production this would call into a training\n    /// service, ship GPU jobs, etc.\n    async fn train_model(\n        &self,\n        model_id: &ModelId,\n        hyper_params: HyperParams,\n        data: DataBatch,\n    ) -> Result<Metrics, ModelOpsError> {\n        info!(\"Training model `{model_id}` with params {:?}\", hyper_params);\n        // Fake training latency\n        sleep(Duration::from_millis(500)).await;\n\n        // Return fake metrics inversely proportional to learning rate\n        let val_accuracy = 0.85\n            + 0.1 * (1e-2 - async fn update(&self, event: MetricsEvent) {\n        let acc = event.metrics.get(\"val_accuracy\").cloned().unwrap_or(1.0);\n        if acc >= self.degradation_threshold {\n            debug!(\n                \"Model `{}` accuracy {:.3} above threshold {:.3}, no action.\",\n                event.model_id, acc, self.degradation_threshold\n            );\n            return;\n        }\n\n        warn!(\n            \"Model `{}` accuracy {:.3} below threshold {:.3}, initiating retraining.\",\n            event.model_id, acc, self.degradation_threshold\n        );\n\n        let registry_clone = self.registry.clone();\n        let tuner_clone = self.tuner.clone();\n        let model_id = event.model_id.clone();\n\n        // Fire-and-forget background task\n        if let Err(e) = observe(&self) -> ObservedDrift {\n            let (tx, rx) = oneshot::channel();\n            *self.notifier.blocking_lock() = Some(tx);\n            ObservedDrift {\n                threshold: self.threshold,\n                retrain_flag: self.retrain_flag.clone(),\n                notifier: rx,\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    pub struct ObservedDrift {\n        threshold: f32,\n        retrain_flag: Arc<AtomicBool>,\n        notifier: oneshot::Receiver<()>,\n    }\n\n    impl ObservedDrift {\n        pub fn emit(&self, evt: Event) {\n            if let Event::Drift(d) = evt {\n                if d.magnitude > self.threshold {\n                    self.retrain_flag.store(true, Ordering::Relaxed);\n                    // Fire the notifier if present\n                    let _ = self.notifier.close();\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        pub async fn on_retrain(&mut self) -> Result<()> {\n            self.notifier.await.ok();\n            if self.retrain_flag.load(Ordering::Relaxed) {\n                Ok(())\n            } else {\n                Err(anyhow::anyhow!(\"retrain flag was never set\"))\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // --------------------------------------------------\n    // Blanket `Observed` impl for `ObservedDrift`\n    // --------------------------------------------------\n    #[async_trait]\n    impl Observed for ObservedDrift {\n        async fn on_event(&mut self, evt: Event) -> Result<()> {\n            self.emit(evt);\n            Ok(())\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Re-export stubs into parent Experiment Tracking Abstraction Layer\n//! =====================================\n//!\n//! This module provides a trait-based abstraction for experiment tracking,\n//! decoupling the core ML training and hyperparameter tuning logic from\n//! concrete tracking implementations.\n//!\n//! # Design Rationale\n//!\n//! The `ExperimentTracker` trait allows for:\n//! - Easy swapping of tracking backends (MLflow, W&B, local files, etc.)\n//! - Mock implementations for testing without I/O\n//! - Clean separation of concerns\n//!\n//! # Usage\n//!\n//! Functions that need to log metrics should accept a generic parameter\n//! bounded by `ExperimentTracker`:\n//!\n//! ```rust\n//! fn train_model<T: ExperimentTracker>(tracker: &T, ...) {\n//!     tracker.log_scalar(\"loss\", 0.5, 1);\n//!     tracker.log_hyperparameter(\"learning_rate\", \"0.001\");\n//! }\n//! ```\n\nuse std::path::Path;\n\nuse crate::RawMetricClient;\n\n/// Trait abstracting experiment tracking operations.\n///\n/// Implementors of this trait can log metrics, hyperparameters, and artifacts\n/// to various backends (local files, MLflow, cloud services, etc.).\npub trait ExperimentTracker: Send + Sync {\n    /// Log a scalar metric value at a given training step.\n    ///\n    /// # Arguments\n    /// * `metric_name` - Name of the metric (e.g., \"loss\", \"accuracy\")\n    /// * `value` - The scalar value to log\n    /// * `step` - The training step or epoch number\n    fn log_scalar(&self, metric_name: &str, value: f64, step: u64);\n\n    /// Log a hyperparameter configuration.\n    ///\n    /// # Arguments\n    /// * `param_name` - Name of the hyperparameter (e.g., \"learning_rate\")\n    /// * `value` - String representation of the hyperparameter value\n    fn log_hyperparameter(&self, param_name: &str, value: &str);\n\n    /// Upload a model artifact to the tracking server.\n    ///\n    /// # Arguments\n    /// * `local_path` - Path to the local file to upload\n    /// * `remote_name` - Name to use for the artifact in the tracking system\n    ///\n    /// # Returns\n    /// * `Ok(())` on successful upload\n    /// * `Err(std::io::Error)` if the upload fails\n    fn upload_model_artifact(&self, local_path: &Path, remote_name: &str) -> Result<(), std::io::Error>;\n}\n\n/// Default implementation of `ExperimentTracker` that wraps `RawMetricClient`.\n///\n/// This struct provides a production-ready implementation that delegates\n/// all tracking operations to the underlying `RawMetricClient`.\npub struct DefaultTracker {\n    client: RawMetricClient,\n}\n\nimpl DefaultTracker {\n    /// Create a new `DefaultTracker` with the specified tracking endpoint.\n    ///\n    /// # Arguments\n    /// * `endpoint` - The URL or connection string for the tracking server\n    pub fn new(endpoint: impl Into<String>) -> Self {\n        Self {\n            client: RawMetricClient::new(endpoint),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nimpl ExperimentTracker for DefaultTracker {\n    fn log_scalar(&self, metric_name: &str, value: f64, step: u64) {\n        self.client.log_scalar(metric_name, value, step);\n    }\n\n    fn log_hyperparameter(&self, param_name: &str, value: &str) {\n        self.client.log_hyperparameter(param_name, value);\n    }\n\n    fn upload_model_artifact(&self, local_path: &Path, remote_name: &str) -> Result<(), std::io::Error> {\n        self.client.upload_model_artifact(local_path, remote_name)\n    }\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n    use std::cell::RefCell;\n    use std::path::PathBuf;\n\n    /// Mock tracker for testing that records all operations.\n    struct MockTracker {\n        scalars: RefCell<Vec<(String, f64, u64)>>,\n        hyperparams: RefCell<Vec<(String, String)>>,\n        artifacts: RefCell<Vec<(PathBuf, String)>>,\n    }\n\n    impl MockTracker {\n        fn new() -> Self {\n            Self {\n                scalars: RefCell::new(Vec::new()),\n                hyperparams: RefCell::new(Vec::new()),\n                artifacts: RefCell::new(Vec::new()),\n            }\n        }\n\n        fn scalar_count(&self) -> usize {\n            self.scalars.borrow().len()\n        }\n\n        fn hyperparam_count(&self) -> usize {\n            self.hyperparams.borrow().len()\n        }\n\n        fn artifact_count(&self) -> usize {\n            self.artifacts.borrow().len()\n        }\n    }\n\n    impl ExperimentTracker for MockTracker {\n        fn log_scalar(&self, metric_name: &str, value: f64, step: u64) {\n            self.scalars.borrow_mut().push((metric_name.to_string(), value, step));\n        }\n\n        fn log_hyperparameter(&self, param_name: &str, value: &str) {\n            self.hyperparams.borrow_mut().push((param_name.to_string(), value.to_string()));\n        }\n\n        fn upload_model_artifact(&self, local_path: &Path, remote_name: &str) -> Result<(), std::io::Error> {\n            self.artifacts.borrow_mut().push((local_path.to_path_buf(), remote_name.to_string()));\n            Ok(())\n        }\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn mock_tracker_records_scalars() {\n        let tracker = MockTracker::new();\n        tracker.log_scalar(\"loss\", 0.5, 1);\n        tracker.log_scalar(\"accuracy\", 0.95, 1);\n        assert_eq!(tracker.scalar_count(), 2);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn mock_tracker_records_hyperparams() {\n        let tracker = MockTracker::new();\n        tracker.log_hyperparameter(\"learning_rate\", \"0.001\");\n        tracker.log_hyperparameter(\"batch_size\", \"32\");\n        assert_eq!(tracker.hyperparam_count(), 2);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn mock_tracker_records_artifacts() {\n        let tracker = MockTracker::new();\n        let path = PathBuf::from(\"/tmp/model.pt\");\n        tracker.upload_model_artifact(&path, \"best_model.pt\").unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(tracker.artifact_count(), 1);\n    }\n}\n```\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":108,"cache_read_input_tokens":59174,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":108,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":1,"service_tier":"standard"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"05b67c7e-d82f-4d1e-a2f0-cce75beca623","uuid":"162d4b6c-4fd4-45ba-883c-f626064d4526"}

This is\n    99→/// intentionally minimal because higher layers might choose to forward these\n   100→/// numbers to Prometheus, StatsD, or another monitoring backend.\n   101→///\n   102→/// Internally it stores *exponential moving averages* so that it can be used\n   103→/// in hot-paths without unbounded memory growth.\n   104→pub struct Metrics {\n   105→    /// Moving average of durations (seconds) per label.\n   106→    durations: std::collections::HashMap<String, f64>,\n   107→    /// EWMA smoothing factor in `[0,1]`.\n   108→    alpha: f64,\n   109→}\n   110→\n   111→impl Metrics {\n   112→    fn new(alpha: f64) -> Self {\n   113→        Self {\n   114→            durations: std::collections::HashMap::new(),\n   115→            alpha,\n   116→        }\n   117→    }\n   118→\n   119→    /// Record a latency sample under `label`.\n   120→    pub fn record_duration(&mut self, label: impl Into<String>, secs: f64) {\n   121→        let label = label.into();\n   122→        let entry = self.durations.entry(label).or_insert(secs);\n   123→        // EWMA: v_t = alpha * x_t + (1-alpha) * v_{t-1}\n   124→        *entry = self.alpha * secs + (1.0 - self.alpha) * *entry;\n   125→    }\n   126→\n   127→    /// Return the current EWMA for `label`, if any.\n   128→    pub fn duration_avg(&self, label: &str) -> Option<f64> {\n   129→        self.durations.get(label).copied()\n   130→    }\n   131→}\n   132→\n   133→/// Static metrics instance; The resulting struct is cached in a global `Lazy`.\n   180→///\n   181→/// Example:\n   182→/// ```no_run\n   183→/// let cfg = visu_tility_utils::config();\n   184→/// println!(\"Schema v{} loaded\", cfg.schema_version);\n   185→/// ```\n   186→fn read_config_from_disk(path: &Path) -> Result<AppConfig> {\n   187→    if !path.exists() {\n   188→        return Err(UtilError::ConfigNotFound(path.into()).into());\n   189→    }\n   190→\n   191→    let mut file =\n   192→        File::open(path).with_context(|| format!(\"opening configuration file `{}`\", path.display()))?;\n   193→\n   194→    let mut buf = Vec::new();\n   195→    file.read_to_end(&mut buf)\n   196→        .with_context(|| format!(\"reading configuration file `{}`\", path.display()))?;\n   197→\n   198→    if buf.len() > MAX_CONFIG_SIZE {\n   199→        return Err(anyhow!(\n   200→            \"configuration file `{}` larger than {} KiB\",\n   201→            path.display(),\n   202→            MAX_CONFIG_SIZE / 1024\n   203→        ));\n   204→    }\n   205→\n   206→    let cfg = match path.extension().and_then(|s| s.to_str()) {\n   207→        Some(\"yaml\") | Some(\"yml\") => serde_yaml::from_slice(&buf)?,\n   208→        Some(\"json\") => serde_json::from_slice(&buf)?,\n   209→        other => {\n   210→            return Err(anyhow!(\n   211→                \"unsupported configuration extension {:?} for `{}`\",\n   212→                other,\n   213→                path.display()\n   214→            ))\n   215→        }\n   216→    };\n   217→\n   218→    Ok(cfg)\n   219→}\n   220→\n   221→/// Discover the config path in the following order:\n   222→///\n   223→/// 1. &'static AppConfig {\n   275→    &CONFIG\n   276→}\n   277→\n   278→/// Generic exponential *back-off* / *retry* helper.\n   279→///\n   280→/// The provided closure is executed until it either succeeds or\n   281→/// max_attempts have been reached.\n   282→///\n   283→/// Usage:\n   284→/// ```ignore\n   285→/// use visu_tility_utils::retry;\n   286→///\n   287→/// let result = retry(\n 300→        match f() {\n   301→            Ok(v) => return Ok(v),\n   302→            Err(e) if attempt + 1 == cfg.max_attempts => return Err(e),\n   303→            Err(_e) => {\n   304→ Every caller receives a *clone* of the receiver\n   405→    /// which means all subscribers see *all* events (fan-out).\n   406→    pub fn subscribe() -> Subscription {\n   407→        BUS.1.clone()\n   408→    }\n   409→}\n   410→\n   411→/// A small proc-macro-like helper (implemented as declarative macro to avoid meta\n   412→/// crate) for scoped timing.\n   413→///\n   414→/// ```\n   415→/// visu_tility_utils::scope_time!(\"fancy_stage\", {\n   416→///     expensive();\n   417→/// });\n   418→/// ```\n   419→#[macro_export]\n   420→macro_rules! scope_time {\n   421→    ($label:expr, $block:block) => {{\n   422→        let _g = $crate::ScopedTimer::new($label);\n   423→        $block\n   424→    }};\n   425→}\n   426→\n   427→/// Tests live inside the same file so that the utils crate remains\n   428→/// a **single** compilation unit.
